# Per-CIP API demonstration

Generated by `demo/scripts/per-cip-curls.sh` from live curl responses. No cached PQS or prior-run bodies are used.

Captured UTC: 2026-10-20T22:12:47.282711+00:00; revision: `8d9a55450beee9c33cafce6ce7e782089c33b20f`; working tree dirty: `True`.
Credentials: `http://localhost:41003`; DID: `http://localhost:42003`; ledger: `http://localhost:41001`; BFT: `http://localhost:42007`.
One Canton participant, one PQS and one PostgreSQL database serve both APIs. Four DID HTTP front doors proxy the one Credentials name authority; they are not independent name authorities or a name-resolution quorum.
Historical note: the 2026-10-18 capture used the old LF HTTP contract. This run uses the VC2 projection; retained volumes from prior in-memory Canton runs are not reused.

## Credentials / VC

### #209 holding: no HTTP demonstration

Objective: identify the boundary for `urn:demo:holding:team.canton`.
Does not prove: that the holding is available over HTTP. `PqsCredentialQueryClient` queries the Credential and RegisteredCredential interfaces only, not the Holding interface or DemoNameHolding template. Exposing holdings over HTTP is a separate approval-dependent slice; no successful curl is claimed.

### DID assertions

Objective: Read the DID-side assertion for Alice's localnet alias.
Does not prove: Does not prove that conflicting aliases are silently resolved.

```sh
curl --silent --show-error --max-time 40 --write-out '
__CURL_METADATA__%{http_code} %{content_type}' 'http://localhost:42003/v1/demo-name-assertions?alias=urn%3Afqpn%3Alocalnet%3Ademo%3Aalice.canton'
```

Content-Type: application/json;charset=UTF-8
curl exit code: 0

## Topology and SQL / check summary

The registry and registered credential share the DSO/PQS projection with DID. The BFT reader checks DID document digest quorum, not independent name authority. Four proxies returned the same forward and reverse responses; conflicting Bob returned 409, unallocated name returned 404 on each. PQS SQL is exercised through the registry, credentials, registered credential, DID, alias and resolution readers; no SQL query plan or independent database audit is claimed.

## Four Credentials hosts and Credentials BFT reader

Objective: read the same seeded VC2 credential from four HTTP hosts and compare full parsed JSON, then read it through the Credentials BFT reader.
Does not prove: independent registries or the full fault matrix. All hosts query the same PQS; the reader fault matrix is unit-level only (`CredentialBftReaderTest`). The healthy live reader path has four agreeing origins; the optional stopped-host probe demonstrates only one real availability fault.

Four Credentials host bodies equal by parsed JSON; one logical registry backed by the same PQS. DID hosts 42003-42006 each forward to their corresponding Credentials host. The DID proxy equality checks above exercise all four forwarding paths.
